 };
 
 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA implementation
+// ============================================================================
+//
+// Mesh and octree construction are intentionally left on the host: they are
+// one-time, irregular setup work.  The all-pairs visibility sampling, dense
+// delay matrix, radiosity propagation, and correlation are all executed on the
+// GPU.  The random values are generated in the same host order as the original
+// mt19937 stream, then consumed independently by CUDA threads.  This keeps the
+// Monte Carlo experiment deterministic while exposing every triangle pair to
+// the GPU in parallel.
+
+struct CudaVec3 {
+    val_t x, y, z;
+};
+
+struct CudaTriangle {
+    CudaVec3 a, b, c;
+    CudaVec3 normal;
+};
+
+struct CudaOctreeNode {
+    CudaVec3 center;
+    CudaVec3 halfExtent;
+    int children[8];
+    uint32_t triangleOffset;
+    uint32_t triangleCount;
+};
+
+constexpr uint32_t NO_RANDOM_SAMPLES = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
+constexpr unsigned int CUDA_THREADS = 256;
+constexpr size_t FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS = 1u << 18;
+
+inline CudaVec3 toCudaVec3(const Vec3& v) {
+    return {v.x, v.y, v.z};
+}
+
+inline CudaTriangle toCudaTriangle(const Triangle& triangle) {
+    return {toCudaVec3(triangle.a), toCudaVec3(triangle.b), toCudaVec3(triangle.c),
+            toCudaVec3(triangle.normal())};
+}
+
+int flattenOctree(const Octree& node, std::vector<CudaOctreeNode>& nodes,
+                  std::vector<uint32_t>& triangleIndices) {
+    const int nodeIndex = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+    CudaOctreeNode flatNode{};
+    flatNode.center = toCudaVec3(node.center);
+    flatNode.halfExtent = toCudaVec3(node.halfExtent);
+    std::fill(std::begin(flatNode.children), std::end(flatNode.children), -1);
+
+    if (!node.triangleIndices.empty()) {
+        if (triangleIndices.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max() ||
+            node.triangleIndices.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "Octree is too large for CUDA triangle indices\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        flatNode.triangleOffset = static_cast<uint32_t>(triangleIndices.size());
+        flatNode.triangleCount = static_cast<uint32_t>(node.triangleIndices.size());
+        for (size_t index : node.triangleIndices) {
+            triangleIndices.push_back(static_cast<uint32_t>(index));
+        }
+    }
+
+    nodes.push_back(flatNode);
+    for (int child = 0; child < 8; ++child) {
+        if (node.children[child]) {
+            const int childIndex = flattenOctree(*node.children[child], nodes, triangleIndices);
+            nodes[nodeIndex].children[child] = childIndex;
+        }
+    }
+    return nodeIndex;
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const char* message) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: %s\n", message);
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+dim3 cudaGridFor(size_t count, unsigned int threads = CUDA_THREADS) {
+    const size_t blocks = (count + threads - 1) / threads;
+    if (blocks > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
+        cudaFailure("kernel grid is too large");
+    }
+    return dim3(static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaAdd(const CudaVec3& a, const CudaVec3& b) {
+    return {a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaSub(const CudaVec3& a, const CudaVec3& b) {
+    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaScale(const CudaVec3& v, val_t scale) {
+    return {v.x * scale, v.y * scale, v.z * scale};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t cudaDot(const CudaVec3& a, const CudaVec3& b) {
+    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaCross(const CudaVec3& a, const CudaVec3& b) {
+    return {a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y,
+            a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
+            a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x};
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t cudaSquaredNorm(const CudaVec3& v) {
+    return cudaDot(v, v);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaTriangleCenter(const CudaTriangle& triangle) {
+    return cudaScale(cudaAdd(cudaAdd(triangle.a, triangle.b), triangle.c), 1.0f / 3.0f);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaRandomPointInTriangle(const CudaTriangle& triangle,
+                                                                val_t u, val_t v) {
+    if (u + v > ONE) {
+        u = ONE - u;
+        v = ONE - v;
+    }
+    return cudaAdd(triangle.a,
+                   cudaAdd(cudaScale(cudaSub(triangle.b, triangle.a), u),
+                           cudaScale(cudaSub(triangle.c, triangle.a), v)));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ val_t cudaRayTriangleIntersect(const CudaVec3& origin,
+                                                            const CudaVec3& direction,
+                                                            const CudaTriangle& triangle) {
+    const CudaVec3 edge1 = cudaSub(triangle.b, triangle.a);
+    const CudaVec3 edge2 = cudaSub(triangle.c, triangle.a);
+    const CudaVec3 pvec = cudaCross(direction, edge2);
+    const val_t determinant = cudaDot(edge1, pvec);
+
+    if (fabsf(determinant) < EPSILON) return 3.402823466e+38F;
+
+    const val_t invDeterminant = ONE / determinant;
+    const CudaVec3 tvec = cudaSub(origin, triangle.a);
+    const val_t u = cudaDot(tvec, pvec) * invDeterminant;
+    if (u < ZERO || u > ONE) return 3.402823466e+38F;
+
+    const CudaVec3 qvec = cudaCross(tvec, edge1);
+    const val_t v = cudaDot(direction, qvec) * invDeterminant;
+    if (v < ZERO || u + v > ONE) return 3.402823466e+38F;
+
+    return cudaDot(edge2, qvec) * invDeterminant;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool cudaRayIntersectsBox(const CudaVec3& p1, const CudaVec3& p2,
+                                                       const CudaOctreeNode& node) {
+    const CudaVec3 d = cudaScale(cudaSub(p2, p1), 0.5f);
+    const CudaVec3 c = cudaSub(cudaAdd(p1, d), node.center);
+    const CudaVec3 ad = {fabsf(d.x), fabsf(d.y), fabsf(d.z)};
+
+    if (fabsf(c.x) > node.halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;
+    if (fabsf(c.y) > node.halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;
+    if (fabsf(c.z) > node.halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;
+
+    if (fabsf(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > node.halfExtent.y * ad.z + node.halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > node.halfExtent.z * ad.x + node.halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > node.halfExtent.x * ad.y + node.halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool cudaRayBlocked(const CudaVec3& from, const CudaVec3& to,
+                                                 const CudaTriangle* triangles,
+                                                 const CudaOctreeNode* nodes,
+                                                 const uint32_t* nodeTriangles,
+                                                 uint32_t sourceTriangle,
+                                                 uint32_t destinationTriangle) {
+    const CudaVec3 direction = cudaSub(to, from);
+    const val_t rayLength = sqrtf(cudaSquaredNorm(direction));
+    if (rayLength < EPSILON) return true;
+    const CudaVec3 normalizedDirection = cudaScale(direction, ONE / rayLength);
+
+    // The constructed octree is shallow (bounded by its 0.5-unit leaf size).
+    // A depth-first traversal needs at most seven deferred siblings per level.
+    int stack[128];
+    int stackSize = 1;
+    stack[0] = 0;
+
+    while (stackSize != 0) {
+        const CudaOctreeNode& node = nodes[stack[--stackSize]];
+        if (node.triangleCount != 0) {
+            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < node.triangleCount; ++k) {
+                const uint32_t triangleIndex = nodeTriangles[node.triangleOffset + k];
+                if (triangleIndex == sourceTriangle || triangleIndex == destinationTriangle) continue;
+
+                const val_t distance = cudaRayTriangleIntersect(
+                    from, normalizedDirection, triangles[triangleIndex]);
+                if (distance > EPSILON && distance < rayLength - EPSILON) return true;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        // Push in reverse order to retain the original recursive 0..7 child order.
+        for (int child = 7; child >= 0; --child) {
+            const int childIndex = node.children[child];
+            if (childIndex >= 0 && cudaRayIntersectsBox(from, to, nodes[childIndex])) {
+                if (stackSize == 128) return false;
+                stack[stackSize++] = childIndex;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+__global__ void computeTimeDelaysKernel(const CudaTriangle* triangles, int* tau,
+                                        size_t numTriangles, size_t pairCount) {
+    const size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (pair >= pairCount) return;
+
+    const size_t i = pair / numTriangles;
+    const size_t j = pair - i * numTriangles;
+    if (i == j) return;
+
+    const CudaVec3 delta = cudaSub(cudaTriangleCenter(triangles[i]), cudaTriangleCenter(triangles[j]));
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(cudaSquaredNorm(delta)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void computeFormFactorsKernel(const CudaTriangle* triangles,
+                                         const CudaOctreeNode* nodes,
+                                         const uint32_t* nodeTriangles,
+                                         const uint32_t* sampleOffsets,
+                                         const val_t* samples,
+                                         val_t* kij,
+                                         size_t numTriangles,
+                                         size_t pairBegin,
+                                         size_t pairCount) {
+    const size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= pairCount) return;
+
+    const uint32_t sampleOffset = sampleOffsets[localPair];
+    if (sampleOffset == NO_RANDOM_SAMPLES) return;
+
+    const size_t pair = pairBegin + localPair;
+    const size_t i = pair / numTriangles;
+    const size_t j = pair - i * numTriangles;
+    const CudaTriangle& receiver = triangles[i];
+    const CudaTriangle& emitter = triangles[j];
+    val_t formFactor = ZERO;
+
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const val_t* raySamples = samples + sampleOffset + ray * 4;
+        const CudaVec3 receiverPoint = cudaRandomPointInTriangle(receiver, raySamples[0], raySamples[1]);
+        const CudaVec3 emitterPoint = cudaRandomPointInTriangle(emitter, raySamples[2], raySamples[3]);
+
+        if (cudaRayBlocked(receiverPoint, emitterPoint, triangles, nodes, nodeTriangles,
+                           static_cast<uint32_t>(i), static_cast<uint32_t>(j))) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const CudaVec3 rayVector = cudaSub(emitterPoint, receiverPoint);
+        const val_t distanceSquared = cudaSquaredNorm(rayVector);
+        if (distanceSquared < EPSILON) continue;
+
+        const val_t inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distanceSquared);
+        const val_t receiverCosine = fmaxf(ZERO, cudaDot(rayVector, receiver.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        const val_t emitterCosine = fmaxf(ZERO, -cudaDot(rayVector, emitter.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (receiverCosine <= ZERO || emitterCosine <= ZERO) continue;
+
+        formFactor += (receiverCosine * emitterCosine) / (PI * distanceSquared);
+    }
+
+    kij[pair] = formFactor * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateRadiosityKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                         const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                         val_t* radB, size_t numTriangles,
+                                         size_t timestep, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                         size_t sourceOffTime) {
+    const size_t receiver = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (receiver >= numTriangles) return;
+
+    val_t sumB = ZERO;
+    const size_t rowOffset = receiver * numTriangles;
+    for (size_t emitter = 0; emitter < numTriangles; ++emitter) {
+        if (receiver == emitter) continue;
+
+        const int delay = tau[rowOffset + emitter];
+        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+
+        const val_t formFactor = kij[rowOffset + emitter];
+        if (formFactor <= ZERO) continue;
+
+        const size_t sourceTime = timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay);
+        const val_t sourceRadiosity = radB[sourceTime * numTriangles + emitter];
+        if (sourceRadiosity <= ZERO) continue;
+
+        sumB += fminf(formFactor * areas[emitter], ONE) * sourceRadiosity;
+    }
+
+    const val_t emission = receiver == sourceIndex && timestep < sourceOffTime ? ONE : ZERO;
+    radB[timestep * numTriangles + receiver] = rho[receiver] * sumB + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void transposeRadiosityKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* byTriangle,
+                                         size_t numTriangles, size_t numTimesteps,
+                                         size_t elementCount) {
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (element >= elementCount) return;
+
+    const size_t timestep = element / numTriangles;
+    const size_t triangle = element - timestep * numTriangles;
+    byTriangle[triangle * numTimesteps + timestep] = radB[element];
+}
+
+__global__ void crossCorrelationKernel(const val_t* byTriangle, val_t* correlations,
+                                       size_t numTriangles, size_t numTimesteps,
+                                       size_t sourceIndex, size_t correlationCount) {
+    const size_t correlation = blockIdx.x;
+    if (correlation >= correlationCount) return;
+
+    const size_t triangle = correlation / numTimesteps;
+    const size_t delay = correlation - triangle * numTimesteps;
+    const val_t* triangleRadiosity = byTriangle + triangle * numTimesteps;
+    const val_t* sourceRadiosity = byTriangle + sourceIndex * numTimesteps;
+
+    val_t partial = ZERO;
+    for (size_t time = delay + threadIdx.x; time < numTimesteps; time += blockDim.x) {
+        partial += sourceRadiosity[time - delay] * triangleRadiosity[time];
+    }
+
+    __shared__ val_t reductions[CUDA_THREADS];
+    reductions[threadIdx.x] = partial;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) reductions[threadIdx.x] += reductions[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) correlations[correlation] = reductions[0];
+}
+
+__global__ void selectDistancesKernel(const val_t* correlations, val_t* distances,
+                                      size_t numTimesteps, size_t numTriangles) {
+    const size_t triangle = blockIdx.x;
+    if (triangle >= numTriangles) return;
+
+    val_t bestCorrelation = ZERO;
+    unsigned int bestTime = 0;
+    const val_t* triangleCorrelations = correlations + triangle * numTimesteps;
+    for (size_t time = threadIdx.x; time < numTimesteps; time += blockDim.x) {
+        const val_t correlation = triangleCorrelations[time];
+        if (correlation > bestCorrelation) {
+            bestCorrelation = correlation;
+            bestTime = static_cast<unsigned int>(time);
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ val_t bestCorrelations[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ unsigned int bestTimes[CUDA_THREADS];
+    bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x] = bestCorrelation;
+    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = bestTime;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (unsigned int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            const val_t candidateCorrelation = bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            const unsigned int candidateTime = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (candidateCorrelation > bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (candidateCorrelation == bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x] &&
+                 candidateTime < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
+                bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x] = candidateCorrelation;
+                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = candidateTime;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        distances[triangle] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTimes[0]);
+    }
+}
+
+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const SimulationState& state)
+        : numTriangles(state.numTriangles), numTimesteps(state.numTimesteps),
+          pairCount(numTriangles * numTriangles), radiosityCount(numTriangles * numTimesteps) {
+        if (numTriangles == 0 || numTimesteps == 0) cudaFailure("simulation dimensions must be non-zero");
+
+        std::vector<CudaTriangle> hostTriangles;
+        hostTriangles.reserve(numTriangles);
+        for (const Triangle& triangle : state.triangles) hostTriangles.push_back(toCudaTriangle(triangle));
+
+        std::vector<CudaOctreeNode> hostNodes;
+        std::vector<uint32_t> hostNodeTriangles;
+        hostNodes.reserve(numTriangles * 2);
+        flattenOctree(state.octree, hostNodes, hostNodeTriangles);
+
+        allocate(&dTriangles, numTriangles, "triangle buffer");
+        allocate(&dNodes, hostNodes.size(), "octree node buffer");
+        allocate(&dNodeTriangles, hostNodeTriangles.size(), "octree triangle-index buffer");
+        allocate(&dAreas, numTriangles, "area buffer");
+        allocate(&dRho, numTriangles, "reflectivity buffer");
+        allocate(&dKij, pairCount, "form-factor buffer");
+        allocate(&dTau, pairCount, "delay buffer");
+        allocate(&dRadB, radiosityCount, "radiosity buffer");
+        allocate(&dDistances, numTriangles, "distance buffer");
+        allocate(&dSampleOffsets, FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS, "form-factor offset buffer");
+        allocate(&dSamples, FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS * NUM_RAYS * 4, "form-factor sample buffer");
+
+        copyToDevice(dTriangles, hostTriangles.data(), numTriangles, "triangle upload");
+        copyToDevice(dNodes, hostNodes.data(), hostNodes.size(), "octree node upload");
+        copyToDevice(dNodeTriangles, hostNodeTriangles.data(), hostNodeTriangles.size(), "octree triangle-index upload");
+        copyToDevice(dAreas, state.areas.data(), numTriangles, "area upload");
+        copyToDevice(dRho, state.rho.data(), numTriangles, "reflectivity upload");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemset(dKij, 0, pairCount * sizeof(val_t)), "form-factor initialization");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemset(dTau, 0, pairCount * sizeof(int)), "delay initialization");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, radiosityCount * sizeof(val_t)), "radiosity initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dDistances);
+        cudaFree(dRadB);
+        cudaFree(dTau);
+        cudaFree(dKij);
+        cudaFree(dRho);
+        cudaFree(dAreas);
+        cudaFree(dSamples);
+        cudaFree(dSampleOffsets);
+        cudaFree(dNodeTriangles);
+        cudaFree(dNodes);
+        cudaFree(dTriangles);
+    }
+
+    void computeTimeDelays() {
+        computeTimeDelaysKernel<<<cudaGridFor(pairCount), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+            dTriangles, dTau, numTriangles, pairCount);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "time-delay kernel launch");
+    }
+
+    void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+        std::vector<uint32_t> sampleOffsets;
+        std::vector<val_t> samples;
+        sampleOffsets.resize(FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS);
+        samples.reserve(FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS * NUM_RAYS * 4);
+        RandomGenerator rng(42);
+
+        for (size_t pairBegin = 0; pairBegin < pairCount; pairBegin += FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS) {
+            const size_t batchPairs = std::min(FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS, pairCount - pairBegin);
+            std::fill(sampleOffsets.begin(), sampleOffsets.begin() + batchPairs, NO_RANDOM_SAMPLES);
+            samples.clear();
+
+            for (size_t localPair = 0; localPair < batchPairs; ++localPair) {
+                const size_t pair = pairBegin + localPair;
+                const size_t i = pair / numTriangles;
+                const size_t j = pair - i * numTriangles;
+                if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
+                    sampleOffsets[localPair] = static_cast<uint32_t>(samples.size());
+                    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+                        samples.push_back(rng.rand());
+                        samples.push_back(rng.rand());
+                        samples.push_back(rng.rand());
+                        samples.push_back(rng.rand());
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (j + 1 == numTriangles &&
+                    ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == numTriangles)) {
+                    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, numTriangles);
+                }
+            }
+
+            copyToDevice(dSampleOffsets, sampleOffsets.data(), batchPairs, "form-factor offset upload");
+            if (!samples.empty()) {
+                copyToDevice(dSamples, samples.data(), samples.size(), "form-factor sample upload");
+            }
+            computeFormFactorsKernel<<<cudaGridFor(batchPairs), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+                dTriangles, dNodes, dNodeTriangles, dSampleOffsets, dSamples, dKij,
+                numTriangles, pairBegin, batchPairs);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "form-factor kernel launch");
+        }
+
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "form-factor kernels");
+        copyToHost(state.tau.data(), dTau, pairCount, "delay download");
+        copyToHost(state.kij.data(), dKij, pairCount, "form-factor download");
+    }
+
+    void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
+        const size_t sourceOffTime = numTimesteps / 2;
+        for (size_t timestep = 0; timestep < numTimesteps; ++timestep) {
+            propagateRadiosityKernel<<<cudaGridFor(numTriangles), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+                dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadB, numTriangles, timestep,
+                state.sourceIndex, sourceOffTime);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "radiosity kernel launch");
+
+            if ((timestep + 1) % 10 == 0 || timestep + 1 == numTimesteps) {
+                printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", timestep + 1, numTimesteps);
+            }
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "radiosity kernels");
+        copyToHost(state.radB.data(), dRadB, radiosityCount, "radiosity download");
+    }
+
+    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
+        val_t* dByTriangle = nullptr;
+        val_t* dCorrelations = nullptr;
+        allocate(&dByTriangle, radiosityCount, "transposed radiosity buffer");
+        allocate(&dCorrelations, radiosityCount, "correlation buffer");
+
+        transposeRadiosityKernel<<<cudaGridFor(radiosityCount), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+            dRadB, dByTriangle, numTriangles, numTimesteps, radiosityCount);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "radiosity transpose kernel launch");
+        crossCorrelationKernel<<<cudaGridFor(radiosityCount, 1), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+            dByTriangle, dCorrelations, numTriangles, numTimesteps, state.sourceIndex, radiosityCount);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "cross-correlation kernel launch");
+        selectDistancesKernel<<<cudaGridFor(numTriangles, 1), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+            dCorrelations, dDistances, numTimesteps, numTriangles);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "distance-selection kernel launch");
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distance kernels");
+
+        copyToHost(state.distances.data(), dDistances, numTriangles, "distance download");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(dCorrelations), "correlation buffer release");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(dByTriangle), "transposed radiosity buffer release");
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <typename T>
+    static void allocate(T** pointer, size_t count, const char* name) {
+        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
+            cudaFailure("CUDA allocation size overflows size_t");
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(pointer, count * sizeof(T)), name);
+    }
+
+    template <typename T>
+    static void copyToDevice(T* destination, const T* source, size_t count, const char* name) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(destination, source, count * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), name);
+    }
+
+    template <typename T>
+    static void copyToHost(T* destination, const T* source, size_t count, const char* name) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(destination, source, count * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), name);
+    }
+
+    size_t numTriangles;
+    size_t numTimesteps;
+    size_t pairCount;
+    size_t radiosityCount;
+    CudaTriangle* dTriangles = nullptr;
+    CudaOctreeNode* dNodes = nullptr;
+    uint32_t* dNodeTriangles = nullptr;
+    val_t* dAreas = nullptr;
+    val_t* dRho = nullptr;
+    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
+    int* dTau = nullptr;
+    val_t* dRadB = nullptr;
+    val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
+    uint32_t* dSampleOffsets = nullptr;
+    val_t* dSamples = nullptr;
+};

   550	// Mesh and octree construction are intentionally left on the host: they are
   551	// one-time, irregular setup work.  The all-pairs visibility sampling, dense
   552	// delay matrix, radiosity propagation, and correlation are all executed on the
   553	// GPU.  The random values are generated in the same host order as the original
   554	// mt19937 stream, then consumed independently by CUDA threads.  This keeps the
   555	// Monte Carlo experiment deterministic while exposing every triangle pair to
   556	// the GPU in parallel.
   557	
   558	struct CudaVec3 {
   559	    val_t x, y, z;
   560	};
   561	
   562	struct CudaTriangle {
   563	    CudaVec3 a, b, c;
   564	    CudaVec3 normal;
   565	};
   566	
   567	struct CudaOctreeNode {
   568	    CudaVec3 center;
   569	    CudaVec3 halfExtent;
   570	    int children[8];
   571	    uint32_t triangleOffset;
   572	    uint32_t triangleCount;
   573	};
   574	
   575	constexpr uint32_t NO_RANDOM_SAMPLES = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
   576	constexpr unsigned int CUDA_THREADS = 256;
   577	constexpr size_t FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS = 1u << 18;
   578	
   579	inline CudaVec3 toCudaVec3(const Vec3& v) {
   580	    return {v.x, v.y, v.z};
   581	}
   582	
   583	inline CudaTriangle toCudaTriangle(const Triangle& triangle) {
   584	    return {toCudaVec3(triangle.a), toCudaVec3(triangle.b), toCudaVec3(triangle.c),
   585	            toCudaVec3(triangle.normal())};
   586	}
   587	
   588	int flattenOctree(const Octree& node, std::vector<CudaOctreeNode>& nodes,
   589	                  std::vector<uint32_t>& triangleIndices) {
   590	    const int nodeIndex = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
   591	    CudaOctreeNode flatNode{};
   592	    flatNode.center = toCudaVec3(node.center);
   593	    flatNode.halfExtent = toCudaVec3(node.halfExtent);
   594	    std::fill(std::begin(flatNode.children), std::end(flatNode.children), -1);
   595	
   596	    if (!node.triangleIndices.empty()) {
   597	        if (triangleIndices.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max() ||
   598	            node.triangleIndices.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
   599	            fprintf(stderr, "Octree is too large for CUDA triangle indices\n");
   600	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   601	        }
   602	        flatNode.triangleOffset = static_cast<uint32_t>(triangleIndices.size());
   603	        flatNode.triangleCount = static_cast<uint32_t>(node.triangleIndices.size());
   604	        for (size_t index : node.triangleIndices) {
   605	            triangleIndices.push_back(static_cast<uint32_t>(index));
   606	        }
   607	    }
   608	
   609	    nodes.push_back(flatNode);
   610	    for (int child = 0; child < 8; ++child) {
   611	        if (node.children[child]) {
   612	            const int childIndex = flattenOctree(*node.children[child], nodes, triangleIndices);
   613	            nodes[nodeIndex].children[child] = childIndex;
   614	        }
   615	    }
   616	    return nodeIndex;
   617	}
   618	
   619	[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const char* message) {
   620	    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: %s\n", message);
   621	    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   622	}
   623	
   624	void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
   625	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
   626	        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
   627	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   628	    }
   629	}
   630	
   631	dim3 cudaGridFor(size_t count, unsigned int threads = CUDA_THREADS) {
   632	    const size_t blocks = (count + threads - 1) / threads;
   633	    if (blocks > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
   634	        cudaFailure("kernel grid is too large");
   635	    }
   636	    return dim3(static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks));
   637	}
   638	
   639	__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaAdd(const CudaVec3& a, const CudaVec3& b) {
   640	    return {a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z};
   641	}
   642	
   643	__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaSub(const CudaVec3& a, const CudaVec3& b) {
   644	    return {a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z};
   645	}
   646	
   647	__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaScale(const CudaVec3& v, val_t scale) {
   648	    return {v.x * scale, v.y * scale, v.z * scale};
   649	}
   650	
   651	__device__ __forceinline__ val_t cudaDot(const CudaVec3& a, const CudaVec3& b) {
   652	    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
   653	}
   654	
   655	__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaCross(const CudaVec3& a, const CudaVec3& b) {
   656	    return {a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y,
   657	            a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
   658	            a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x};
   659	}
   660	
   661	__device__ __forceinline__ val_t cudaSquaredNorm(const CudaVec3& v) {
   662	    return cudaDot(v, v);
   663	}
   664	
   665	__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaTriangleCenter(const CudaTriangle& triangle) {
   666	    return cudaScale(cudaAdd(cudaAdd(triangle.a, triangle.b), triangle.c), 1.0f / 3.0f);
   667	}
   668	
   669	__device__ __forceinline__ CudaVec3 cudaRandomPointInTriangle(const CudaTriangle& triangle,
   670	                                                                val_t u, val_t v) {
   671	    if (u + v > ONE) {
   672	        u = ONE - u;
   673	        v = ONE - v;
   674	    }
   675	    return cudaAdd(triangle.a,
   676	                   cudaAdd(cudaScale(cudaSub(triangle.b, triangle.a), u),
   677	                           cudaScale(cudaSub(triangle.c, triangle.a), v)));
   678	}
   679	
   680	__device__ __forceinline__ val_t cudaRayTriangleIntersect(const CudaVec3& origin,
   681	                                                            const CudaVec3& direction,
   682	                                                            const CudaTriangle& triangle) {
   683	    const CudaVec3 edge1 = cudaSub(triangle.b, triangle.a);
   684	    const CudaVec3 edge2 = cudaSub(triangle.c, triangle.a);
   685	    const CudaVec3 pvec = cudaCross(direction, edge2);
   686	    const val_t determinant = cudaDot(edge1, pvec);
   687	
   688	    if (fabsf(determinant) < EPSILON) return 3.402823466e+38F;
   689	
   690	    const val_t invDeterminant = ONE / determinant;
   691	    const CudaVec3 tvec = cudaSub(origin, triangle.a);
   692	    const val_t u = cudaDot(tvec, pvec) * invDeterminant;
   693	    if (u < ZERO || u > ONE) return 3.402823466e+38F;
   694	
   695	    const CudaVec3 qvec = cudaCross(tvec, edge1);
   696	    const val_t v = cudaDot(direction, qvec) * invDeterminant;
   697	    if (v < ZERO || u + v > ONE) return 3.402823466e+38F;
   698	
   699	    return cudaDot(edge2, qvec) * invDeterminant;
   700	}
   701	
   702	__device__ __forceinline__ bool cudaRayIntersectsBox(const CudaVec3& p1, const CudaVec3& p2,
   703	                                                       const CudaOctreeNode& node) {
   704	    const CudaVec3 d = cudaScale(cudaSub(p2, p1), 0.5f);
   705	    const CudaVec3 c = cudaSub(cudaAdd(p1, d), node.center);
   706	    const CudaVec3 ad = {fabsf(d.x), fabsf(d.y), fabsf(d.z)};
   707	
   708	    if (fabsf(c.x) > node.halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;
   709	    if (fabsf(c.y) > node.halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;
   710	    if (fabsf(c.z) > node.halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;
   711	
   712	    if (fabsf(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > node.halfExtent.y * ad.z + node.halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;
   713	    if (fabsf(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > node.halfExtent.z * ad.x + node.halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;
   714	    if (fabsf(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > node.halfExtent.x * ad.y + node.halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;
   715	
   716	    return true;
   717	}
   718	
   719	__device__ __forceinline__ bool cudaRayBlocked(const CudaVec3& from, const CudaVec3& to,
   720	                                                 const CudaTriangle* triangles,
   721	                                                 const CudaOctreeNode* nodes,
   722	                                                 const uint32_t* nodeTriangles,
   723	                                                 uint32_t sourceTriangle,
   724	                                                 uint32_t destinationTriangle) {
   725	    const CudaVec3 direction = cudaSub(to, from);
   726	    const val_t rayLength = sqrtf(cudaSquaredNorm(direction));
   727	    if (rayLength < EPSILON) return true;
   728	    const CudaVec3 normalizedDirection = cudaScale(direction, ONE / rayLength);
   729	
   730	    // The constructed octree is shallow (bounded by its 0.5-unit leaf size).
   731	    // A depth-first traversal needs at most seven deferred siblings per level.
   732	    int stack[128];
   733	    int stackSize = 1;
   734	    stack[0] = 0;
   735	
   736	    while (stackSize != 0) {
   737	        const CudaOctreeNode& node = nodes[stack[--stackSize]];
   738	        if (node.triangleCount != 0) {
   739	            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < node.triangleCount; ++k) {
   740	                const uint32_t triangleIndex = nodeTriangles[node.triangleOffset + k];
   741	                if (triangleIndex == sourceTriangle || triangleIndex == destinationTriangle) continue;
   742	
   743	                const val_t distance = cudaRayTriangleIntersect(
   744	                    from, normalizedDirection, triangles[triangleIndex]);
   745	                if (distance > EPSILON && distance < rayLength - EPSILON) return true;
   746	            }
   747	            continue;
   748	        }
   749	
   750	        // Push in reverse order to retain the original recursive 0..7 child order.
   751	        for (int child = 7; child >= 0; --child) {
   752	            const int childIndex = node.children[child];
   753	            if (childIndex >= 0 && cudaRayIntersectsBox(from, to, nodes[childIndex])) {
   754	                if (stackSize == 128) return false;
   755	                stack[stackSize++] = childIndex;
   756	            }
   757	        }
   758	    }
   759	    return false;
   760	}
   761	
   762	__global__ void computeTimeDelaysKernel(const CudaTriangle* triangles, int* tau,
   763	                                        size_t numTriangles, size_t pairCount) {
   764	    const size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   765	    if (pair >= pairCount) return;
   766	
   767	    const size_t i = pair / numTriangles;
   768	    const size_t j = pair - i * numTriangles;
   769	    if (i == j) return;
   770	
   771	    const CudaVec3 delta = cudaSub(cudaTriangleCenter(triangles[i]), cudaTriangleCenter(triangles[j]));
   772	    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(cudaSquaredNorm(delta)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
   773	}
   774	
   775	__global__ void computeFormFactorsKernel(const CudaTriangle* triangles,
   776	                                         const CudaOctreeNode* nodes,
   777	                                         const uint32_t* nodeTriangles,
   778	                                         const uint32_t* sampleOffsets,
   779	                                         const val_t* samples,
   780	                                         val_t* kij,
   781	                                         size_t numTriangles,
   782	                                         size_t pairBegin,
   783	                                         size_t pairCount) {
   784	    const size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   785	    if (localPair >= pairCount) return;
   786	
   787	    const uint32_t sampleOffset = sampleOffsets[localPair];
   788	    if (sampleOffset == NO_RANDOM_SAMPLES) return;
   789	
   790	    const size_t pair = pairBegin + localPair;
   791	    const size_t i = pair / numTriangles;
   792	    const size_t j = pair - i * numTriangles;
   793	    const CudaTriangle& receiver = triangles[i];
   794	    const CudaTriangle& emitter = triangles[j];
   795	    val_t formFactor = ZERO;
   796	
   797	    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
   798	        const val_t* raySamples = samples + sampleOffset + ray * 4;
   799	        const CudaVec3 receiverPoint = cudaRandomPointInTriangle(receiver, raySamples[0], raySamples[1]);
   800	        const CudaVec3 emitterPoint = cudaRandomPointInTriangle(emitter, raySamples[2], raySamples[3]);
   801	
   802	        if (cudaRayBlocked(receiverPoint, emitterPoint, triangles, nodes, nodeTriangles,
   803	                           static_cast<uint32_t>(i), static_cast<uint32_t>(j))) {
   804	            continue;
   805	        }
   806	
   807	        const CudaVec3 rayVector = cudaSub(emitterPoint, receiverPoint);
   808	        const val_t distanceSquared = cudaSquaredNorm(rayVector);
   809	        if (distanceSquared < EPSILON) continue;
   810	
   811	        const val_t inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distanceSquared);
   812	        const val_t receiverCosine = fmaxf(ZERO, cudaDot(rayVector, receiver.normal) * inverseDistance);
   813	        const val_t emitterCosine = fmaxf(ZERO, -cudaDot(rayVector, emitter.normal) * inverseDistance);
   814	        if (receiverCosine <= ZERO || emitterCosine <= ZERO) continue;
   815	
   816	        formFactor += (receiverCosine * emitterCosine) / (PI * distanceSquared);
   817	    }
   818	
   819	    kij[pair] = formFactor * INV_NUM_RAYS;
   820	}
   821	
   822	__global__ void propagateRadiosityKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
   823	                                         const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
   824	                                         val_t* radB, size_t numTriangles,
   825	                                         size_t timestep, size_t sourceIndex,
   826	                                         size_t sourceOffTime) {
   827	    const size_t receiver = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   828	    if (receiver >= numTriangles) return;
   829	
   830	    val_t sumB = ZERO;
   831	    const size_t rowOffset = receiver * numTriangles;
   832	    for (size_t emitter = 0; emitter < numTriangles; ++emitter) {
   833	        if (receiver == emitter) continue;
   834	
   835	        const int delay = tau[rowOffset + emitter];
   836	        if (timestep < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
   837	
   838	        const val_t formFactor = kij[rowOffset + emitter];
   839	        if (formFactor <= ZERO) continue;
   840	
   841	        const size_t sourceTime = timestep - static_cast<size_t>(delay);
   842	        const val_t sourceRadiosity = radB[sourceTime * numTriangles + emitter];
   843	        if (sourceRadiosity <= ZERO) continue;
   844	
   845	        sumB += fminf(formFactor * areas[emitter], ONE) * sourceRadiosity;
   846	    }
   847	
   848	    const val_t emission = receiver == sourceIndex && timestep < sourceOffTime ? ONE : ZERO;
   849	    radB[timestep * numTriangles + receiver] = rho[receiver] * sumB + emission;
   850	}
   851	
   852	__global__ void transposeRadiosityKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* byTriangle,
   853	                                         size_t numTriangles, size_t numTimesteps,
   854	                                         size_t elementCount) {
   855	    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   856	    if (element >= elementCount) return;
   857	
   858	    const size_t timestep = element / numTriangles;
   859	    const size_t triangle = element - timestep * numTriangles;
   860	    byTriangle[triangle * numTimesteps + timestep] = radB[element];
   861	}
   862	
   863	__global__ void crossCorrelationKernel(const val_t* byTriangle, val_t* correlations,
   864	                                       size_t numTriangles, size_t numTimesteps,
   865	                                       size_t sourceIndex, size_t correlationCount) {
   866	    const size_t correlation = blockIdx.x;
   867	    if (correlation >= correlationCount) return;
   868	
   869	    const size_t triangle = correlation / numTimesteps;
   870	    const size_t delay = correlation - triangle * numTimesteps;
   871	    const val_t* triangleRadiosity = byTriangle + triangle * numTimesteps;
   872	    const val_t* sourceRadiosity = byTriangle + sourceIndex * numTimesteps;
   873	
   874	    val_t partial = ZERO;
   875	    for (size_t time = delay + threadIdx.x; time < numTimesteps; time += blockDim.x) {
   876	        partial += sourceRadiosity[time - delay] * triangleRadiosity[time];
   877	    }
   878	
   879	    __shared__ val_t reductions[CUDA_THREADS];
   880	    reductions[threadIdx.x] = partial;
   881	    __syncthreads();
   882	    for (unsigned int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
   883	        if (threadIdx.x < stride) reductions[threadIdx.x] += reductions[threadIdx.x + stride];
   884	        __syncthreads();
   885	    }
   886	    if (threadIdx.x == 0) correlations[correlation] = reductions[0];
   887	}
   888	
   889	__global__ void selectDistancesKernel(const val_t* correlations, val_t* distances,
   890	                                      size_t numTimesteps, size_t numTriangles) {
   891	    const size_t triangle = blockIdx.x;
   892	    if (triangle >= numTriangles) return;
   893	
   894	    val_t bestCorrelation = ZERO;
   895	    unsigned int bestTime = 0;
   896	    const val_t* triangleCorrelations = correlations + triangle * numTimesteps;
   897	    for (size_t time = threadIdx.x; time < numTimesteps; time += blockDim.x) {
   898	        const val_t correlation = triangleCorrelations[time];
   899	        if (correlation > bestCorrelation) {
   900	            bestCorrelation = correlation;
   901	            bestTime = static_cast<unsigned int>(time);
   902	        }
   903	    }
   904	
   905	    __shared__ val_t bestCorrelations[CUDA_THREADS];
   906	    __shared__ unsigned int bestTimes[CUDA_THREADS];
   907	    bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x] = bestCorrelation;
   908	    bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = bestTime;
   909	    __syncthreads();
   910	
   911	    for (unsigned int stride = CUDA_THREADS / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
   912	        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
   913	            const val_t candidateCorrelation = bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x + stride];
   914	            const unsigned int candidateTime = bestTimes[threadIdx.x + stride];
   915	            if (candidateCorrelation > bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x] ||
   916	                (candidateCorrelation == bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x] &&
   917	                 candidateTime < bestTimes[threadIdx.x])) {
   918	                bestCorrelations[threadIdx.x] = candidateCorrelation;
   919	                bestTimes[threadIdx.x] = candidateTime;
   920	            }
   921	        }
   922	        __syncthreads();
   923	    }
   924	
   925	    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
   926	        distances[triangle] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestTimes[0]);
   927	    }
   928	}
   929	
   930	class CudaSimulation {
   931	public:
   932	    explicit CudaSimulation(const SimulationState& state)
   933	        : numTriangles(state.numTriangles), numTimesteps(state.numTimesteps),
   934	          pairCount(numTriangles * numTriangles), radiosityCount(numTriangles * numTimesteps) {
   935	        if (numTriangles == 0 || numTimesteps == 0) cudaFailure("simulation dimensions must be non-zero");
   936	
   937	        std::vector<CudaTriangle> hostTriangles;
   938	        hostTriangles.reserve(numTriangles);
   939	        for (const Triangle& triangle : state.triangles) hostTriangles.push_back(toCudaTriangle(triangle));
   940	
   941	        std::vector<CudaOctreeNode> hostNodes;
   942	        std::vector<uint32_t> hostNodeTriangles;
   943	        hostNodes.reserve(numTriangles * 2);
   944	        flattenOctree(state.octree, hostNodes, hostNodeTriangles);
   945	
   946	        allocate(&dTriangles, numTriangles, "triangle buffer");
   947	        allocate(&dNodes, hostNodes.size(), "octree node buffer");
   948	        allocate(&dNodeTriangles, hostNodeTriangles.size(), "octree triangle-index buffer");
   949	        allocate(&dAreas, numTriangles, "area buffer");
   950	        allocate(&dRho, numTriangles, "reflectivity buffer");
   951	        allocate(&dKij, pairCount, "form-factor buffer");
   952	        allocate(&dTau, pairCount, "delay buffer");
   953	        allocate(&dRadB, radiosityCount, "radiosity buffer");
   954	        allocate(&dDistances, numTriangles, "distance buffer");
   955	        allocate(&dSampleOffsets, FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS, "form-factor offset buffer");
   956	        allocate(&dSamples, FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS * NUM_RAYS * 4, "form-factor sample buffer");
   957	
   958	        copyToDevice(dTriangles, hostTriangles.data(), numTriangles, "triangle upload");
   959	        copyToDevice(dNodes, hostNodes.data(), hostNodes.size(), "octree node upload");
   960	        copyToDevice(dNodeTriangles, hostNodeTriangles.data(), hostNodeTriangles.size(), "octree triangle-index upload");
   961	        copyToDevice(dAreas, state.areas.data(), numTriangles, "area upload");
   962	        copyToDevice(dRho, state.rho.data(), numTriangles, "reflectivity upload");
   963	        checkCuda(cudaMemset(dKij, 0, pairCount * sizeof(val_t)), "form-factor initialization");
   964	        checkCuda(cudaMemset(dTau, 0, pairCount * sizeof(int)), "delay initialization");
   965	        checkCuda(cudaMemset(dRadB, 0, radiosityCount * sizeof(val_t)), "radiosity initialization");
   966	    }
   967	
   968	    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
   969	    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
   970	
   971	    ~CudaSimulation() {
   972	        cudaFree(dDistances);
   973	        cudaFree(dRadB);
   974	        cudaFree(dTau);
   975	        cudaFree(dKij);
   976	        cudaFree(dRho);
   977	        cudaFree(dAreas);
   978	        cudaFree(dSamples);
   979	        cudaFree(dSampleOffsets);
   980	        cudaFree(dNodeTriangles);
   981	        cudaFree(dNodes);
   982	        cudaFree(dTriangles);
   983	    }
   984	
   985	    void computeTimeDelays() {
   986	        computeTimeDelaysKernel<<<cudaGridFor(pairCount), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
   987	            dTriangles, dTau, numTriangles, pairCount);
   988	        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "time-delay kernel launch");
   989	    }
   990	
   991	    void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
   992	        std::vector<uint32_t> sampleOffsets;
   993	        std::vector<val_t> samples;
   994	        sampleOffsets.resize(FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS);
   995	        samples.reserve(FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS * NUM_RAYS * 4);
   996	        RandomGenerator rng(42);
   997	
   998	        for (size_t pairBegin = 0; pairBegin < pairCount; pairBegin += FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS) {
   999	            const size_t batchPairs = std::min(FORM_FACTOR_BATCH_PAIRS, pairCount - pairBegin);
  1000	            std::fill(sampleOffsets.begin(), sampleOffsets.begin() + batchPairs, NO_RANDOM_SAMPLES);
  1001	            samples.clear();
  1002	
  1003	            for (size_t localPair = 0; localPair < batchPairs; ++localPair) {
  1004	                const size_t pair = pairBegin + localPair;
  1005	                const size_t i = pair / numTriangles;
  1006	                const size_t j = pair - i * numTriangles;
  1007	                if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
  1008	                    sampleOffsets[localPair] = static_cast<uint32_t>(samples.size());
  1009	                    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
  1010	                        samples.push_back(rng.rand());
  1011	                        samples.push_back(rng.rand());
  1012	                        samples.push_back(rng.rand());
  1013	                        samples.push_back(rng.rand());
  1014	                    }
  1015	                }
  1016	
  1017	                if (j + 1 == numTriangles &&
  1018	                    ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == numTriangles)) {
  1019	                    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, numTriangles);
  1020	                }
  1021	            }
  1022	
  1023	            copyToDevice(dSampleOffsets, sampleOffsets.data(), batchPairs, "form-factor offset upload");
  1024	            if (!samples.empty()) {
  1025	                copyToDevice(dSamples, samples.data(), samples.size(), "form-factor sample upload");
  1026	            }
  1027	            computeFormFactorsKernel<<<cudaGridFor(batchPairs), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
  1028	                dTriangles, dNodes, dNodeTriangles, dSampleOffsets, dSamples, dKij,
  1029	                numTriangles, pairBegin, batchPairs);
  1030	            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "form-factor kernel launch");
  1031	        }
  1032	
  1033	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "form-factor kernels");
  1034	        copyToHost(state.tau.data(), dTau, pairCount, "delay download");
  1035	        copyToHost(state.kij.data(), dKij, pairCount, "form-factor download");
  1036	    }
  1037	
  1038	    void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
  1039	        const size_t sourceOffTime = numTimesteps / 2;
  1040	        for (size_t timestep = 0; timestep < numTimesteps; ++timestep) {
  1041	            propagateRadiosityKernel<<<cudaGridFor(numTriangles), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
  1042	                dKij, dTau, dAreas, dRho, dRadB, numTriangles, timestep,
  1043	                state.sourceIndex, sourceOffTime);
  1044	            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "radiosity kernel launch");
  1045	
  1046	            if ((timestep + 1) % 10 == 0 || timestep + 1 == numTimesteps) {
  1047	                printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", timestep + 1, numTimesteps);
  1048	            }
  1049	        }
  1050	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "radiosity kernels");
  1051	        copyToHost(state.radB.data(), dRadB, radiosityCount, "radiosity download");
  1052	    }
  1053	
  1054	    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
  1055	        val_t* dByTriangle = nullptr;
  1056	        val_t* dCorrelations = nullptr;
  1057	        allocate(&dByTriangle, radiosityCount, "transposed radiosity buffer");
  1058	        allocate(&dCorrelations, radiosityCount, "correlation buffer");
  1059	
  1060	        transposeRadiosityKernel<<<cudaGridFor(radiosityCount), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
  1061	            dRadB, dByTriangle, numTriangles, numTimesteps, radiosityCount);
  1062	        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "radiosity transpose kernel launch");
  1063	        crossCorrelationKernel<<<cudaGridFor(radiosityCount, 1), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
  1064	            dByTriangle, dCorrelations, numTriangles, numTimesteps, state.sourceIndex, radiosityCount);
  1065	        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "cross-correlation kernel launch");
  1066	        selectDistancesKernel<<<cudaGridFor(numTriangles, 1), CUDA_THREADS>>>(
  1067	            dCorrelations, dDistances, numTimesteps, numTriangles);
  1068	        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "distance-selection kernel launch");
  1069	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "distance kernels");
  1070	
  1071	        copyToHost(state.distances.data(), dDistances, numTriangles, "distance download");
  1072	        checkCuda(cudaFree(dCorrelations), "correlation buffer release");
  1073	        checkCuda(cudaFree(dByTriangle), "transposed radiosity buffer release");
  1074	    }
  1075	
  1076	private:
  1077	    template <typename T>
  1078	    static void allocate(T** pointer, size_t count, const char* name) {
  1079	        if (count > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(T)) {
  1080	            cudaFailure("CUDA allocation size overflows size_t");
  1081	        }
  1082	        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(pointer, count * sizeof(T)), name);
  1083	    }
  1084	
  1085	    template <typename T>
  1086	    static void copyToDevice(T* destination, const T* source, size_t count, const char* name) {
  1087	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(destination, source, count * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), name);
  1088	    }
  1089	
  1090	    template <typename T>
  1091	    static void copyToHost(T* destination, const T* source, size_t count, const char* name) {
  1092	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(destination, source, count * sizeof(T), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), name);
  1093	    }
  1094	
  1095	    size_t numTriangles;
  1096	    size_t numTimesteps;
  1097	    size_t pairCount;
  1098	    size_t radiosityCount;
  1099	    CudaTriangle* dTriangles = nullptr;
  1100	    CudaOctreeNode* dNodes = nullptr;
  1101	    uint32_t* dNodeTriangles = nullptr;
  1102	    val_t* dAreas = nullptr;
  1103	    val_t* dRho = nullptr;
  1104	    val_t* dKij = nullptr;
  1105	    int* dTau = nullptr;
  1106	    val_t* dRadB = nullptr;
  1107	    val_t* dDistances = nullptr;
  1108	    uint32_t* dSampleOffsets = nullptr;
  1109	    val_t* dSamples = nullptr;
  1110	};
